## SpecStory

## 다중 에이전트 트레이딩 시스템 구조 (12/22/2024, 11:19:33 AM)

_**User**_

아래는 **다중 에이전트(Multi-Agent) 트레이딩 시스템**을 구축하기 위해 권장하는 디렉토리 구조와, 필요한 파일 및 구현 방향을 좀 더 구체적으로 정리한 제안안이야. 이미 어느 정도 골격이 잡혀 있지만, 앞으로 다중 에이전트 전략을 확장할 때 더욱 체계적으로 유지·관리하기 위한 아이디어를 담았어.

---

## 1. 전체적인 디렉토리 구조

```
trading_bot/
├── envs/
│   ├── base_env.py               # (선택) 단일/다중 에이전트 환경의 공통 로직 정의
│   ├── single_agent_env.py       # 단일 에이전트 환경 (기존 trading_env.py와 동일 or rename)
│   └── multi_agent_env.py        # 다중 에이전트 환경
│
├── agents/
│   ├── strategies/
│   │   ├── single/
│   │   │   └── ppo_agent.py      # 단일 에이전트 PPO 전략
│   │   └── multi/
│   │       ├── momentum_ppo_agent.py      # 예시: 모멘텀 전략 PPO
│   │       ├── mean_reversion_ppo_agent.py# 예시: 역추세 전략 PPO
│   │       ├── market_maker_ppo_agent.py  # 예시: 마켓메이커 전략 PPO
│   │       └── multi_agent_manager.py     # 여러 에이전트를 합쳐 학습/동작 제어
│   ├── models/
│   │   └── architectures/        # 신경망 구조(MLP, CNN, Transformer 등) 정의
│   └── agent_factory.py          # config 기반으로 에이전트 생성/관리 로직
│
├── models/
│   ├── single_agent/             # 단일 에이전트 모델 체크포인트
│   └── multi_agent/              # 다중 에이전트 모델 체크포인트
│
├── training/
│   ├── train_single.py           # 단일 에이전트 학습 스크립트
│   ├── train_multi.py            # 다중 에이전트 학습 스크립트
│   ├── evaluation.py             # 성능 평가/백테스트 로직
│   ├── utils/                    # 학습/평가 관련 유틸 모듈
│   └── ...
│
└── README.md
```

### 디렉토리별 역할 요약

1. **envs/**  
   - `single_agent_env.py`: 기존 `trading_env.py` 역할로, 단일 에이전트 환경  
   - `multi_agent_env.py`: 여러 에이전트가 동시에 액션을 취하고, 상호작용 결과를 반영하는 환경  
   - `base_env.py`(선택): 단일/다중 에이전트 환경에서 공통으로 쓰이는 함수나 로직을 모아두면 유용

2. **agents/strategies/**  
   - `single/`: 단일 에이전트용 PPO, DDPG, A2C 등 전략들  
   - `multi/`: 다중 에이전트용 전략들  
     - ex) `momentum_ppo_agent.py`, `mean_reversion_ppo_agent.py` 등 전략별 파일  
     - `multi_agent_manager.py`: 여러 에이전트를 한꺼번에 관리(협력/경쟁 메커니즘, 경험 공유, 파라미터 동기화)

3. **agents/models/architectures/**  
   - 신경망 구조(MLP, CNN, LSTM, Transformer 등)에 관한 코드.  
   - PPO 정책망이 어떤 네트워크를 쓸지, 에이전트별로 커스텀 네트워크를 설정할 수도 있음.

4. **models/**  
   - 학습된 모델 체크포인트 저장 폴더.  
   - `single_agent/` vs `multi_agent/`로 분리해둬서 혼동 없이 버전 관리 가능.

5. **training/**  
   - 학습 스크립트(`train_single.py`, `train_multi.py`)와 평가 스크립트(`evaluation.py`)가 위치.  
   - 필요 시 하이퍼파라미터 튜닝 스크립트(`hyperparameter_search.py` 등) 포함.  
   - `utils/` 안에 로그 관리, Callbacks, 스케줄러, 보조함수 등을 넣어두면 깔끔.

---

## 2. 다중 에이전트 환경 vs 전략 구현

### (A) multi_agent_env.py

```python
# envs/multi_agent_env.py

import gym
import numpy as np

class MultiAgentTradingEnv(gym.Env):
    def __init__(self, env_config):
        super().__init__()
        # 관측, 행동 공간 정의
        self.num_agents = env_config.get("num_agents", 2)
        self.observation_space = ...
        self.action_space = ...
        
        # 에이전트 간 상호작용에 필요한 변수
        self.shared_liquidity_pool = ...
        self.market_state = ...
        # etc.

    def reset(self):
        # 모든 에이전트의 초기화
        # 시장 상태 초기화
        ...
        return [obs_1, obs_2, ..., obs_n]

    def step(self, actions):
        # actions: 여러 에이전트가 동시에 낸 액션 리스트/딕셔너리
        # 1) 시장 환경 업데이트
        # 2) 각 에이전트별 보상 계산
        # 3) 관측값, 보상, done 여부 구성
        ...
        return next_observations, rewards, done, info
```

- 에이전트가 여러 개 동시 학습하려면 **actions**도 에이전트별로 받아야 하고, 보상도 다중으로 계산해야 함.  
- `info` 딕셔너리에 에이전트별 보조 정보를 담아줄 수도 있음.

### (B) multi_agent_manager.py (에이전트 관리)

```python
# agents/strategies/multi/multi_agent_manager.py

class MultiAgentManager:
    def __init__(self, agent_configs):
        """
        agent_configs: [
          { "id": "agentA", "strategy": "momentum", ... },
          { "id": "agentB", "strategy": "mean_reversion", ... }
        ]
        """
        self.agents = {}
        for cfg in agent_configs:
            if cfg["strategy"] == "momentum":
                self.agents[cfg["id"]] = MomentumPPOAgent(cfg)
            elif cfg["strategy"] == "mean_reversion":
                self.agents[cfg["id"]] = MeanReversionPPOAgent(cfg)
            # ...
    
    def act(self, observations):
        """
        observations: {"agentA": obsA, "agentB": obsB, ...}
        return: actions {"agentA": actA, "agentB": actB, ...}
        """
        actions = {}
        for agent_id, obs in observations.items():
            actions[agent_id] = self.agents[agent_id].act(obs)
        return actions

    def train_step(self, experiences):
        """
        experiences: 각 에이전트별 (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) 샘플
        다중 에이전트 학습 로직
        """
        for agent_id, exp in experiences.items():
            self.agents[agent_id].update(exp)
        # 필요하다면 에이전트 간 공유/협업/경쟁 로직

    def save(self, path):
        # 전체 다중 에이전트의 파라미터를 저장
        for agent_id, agent in self.agents.items():
            agent.save(path + f"/{agent_id}")
```

- `MultiAgentManager`가 **각 에이전트의 초기화**, 학습 루프, 업데이트, 모델 세이브/로드**를 담당.  
- **경험 공유**나 **협력/경쟁 로직**을 중앙에서 제어하려면 여기서 구현 가능.

### (C) 개별 에이전트 (MomentumPPOAgent, MeanReversionPPOAgent 등)

```python
# agents/strategies/multi/momentum_ppo_agent.py

class MomentumPPOAgent:
    def __init__(self, config):
        self.gamma = config.get("gamma", 0.99)
        self.lr = config.get("lr", 3e-4)
        # etc.

    def act(self, observation):
        # observation에 기반해 액션 결정
        # PPO 정책망 이용
        ...
        return action

    def update(self, experience):
        # PPO 업데이트 로직
        # advantage 계산, gradient 업데이트
        ...
    
    def save(self, path):
        # 모델 파라미터 저장
        ...
```

- 각 전략별로 **PPO 정책망의 일부 하이퍼파라미터**나 **인풋 피처**를 차별화해줄 수 있음.  
- 모멘텀 전략: 모멘텀 지표(ROC, RSI 등)에 가중치 반영 / 역추세 전략: RSI 역해석, Bollinger Bands 반영 등.

---

## 3. 학습 스크립트 (train_multi.py)

```python
# training/train_multi.py

from envs.multi_agent_env import MultiAgentTradingEnv
from agents.strategies.multi.multi_agent_manager import MultiAgentManager

def train_multi_agent():
    env_config = {...}
    env = MultiAgentTradingEnv(env_config)
    
    agent_configs = [
        {"id": "agentA", "strategy": "momentum", "lr": 3e-4, ...},
        {"id": "agentB", "strategy": "mean_reversion", "lr": 1e-4, ...},
        # ...
    ]
    manager = MultiAgentManager(agent_configs)

    # 다중 에이전트 학습 루프
    for episode in range(num_episodes):
        obs = env.reset()
        done = False
        while not done:
            actions = manager.act(obs)  # dict 형태
            next_obs, rewards, done, info = env.step(actions)
            
            # 경험을 에이전트별로 정리
            experiences = {}
            for agent_id in actions.keys():
                experiences[agent_id] = {
                    "obs": obs[agent_id],
                    "action": actions[agent_id],
                    "reward": rewards[agent_id],
                    "next_obs": next_obs[agent_id],
                    "done": done
                }
            manager.train_step(experiences)
            
            obs = next_obs

        # 에피소드 끝난 뒤 로그/메트릭 기록, 체크포인트 저장
        if (episode+1) % save_freq == 0:
            manager.save(f"models/multi_agent/episode_{episode+1}")
```

- 다중 에이전트 학습에서는 **관측 obs, 행동 actions, 보상 rewards**가 모두 에이전트별로 dict 형태로 관리되기 쉽다.  
- `manager.act()`, `manager.train_step()`를 통해 **중앙집중식**으로 에이전트를 동시 학습.

---

## 4. 추가 구현 아이디어

1. **경험 공유(Experience Sharing)**  
   - 에이전트가 얻은 transition을 공통 메모리 버퍼에 넣어, 다른 에이전트도 참고하게 하는 구조(협력)  
   - 또는 경쟁 상황에서는 각자 독립 버퍼를 유지하되, 특정 이벤트에 한해서만 정보를 교환.

2. **메타 에이전트(Meta-Agent)**  
   - 여러 하위 에이전트(모멘텀, 역추세, 마켓메이커 등)를 관리하고, 상황에 따라 어느 에이전트의 정책을 쓸지 결정.  
   - `multi_agent_manager.py` 내부에서 메타 정책을 학습할 수도 있음.

3. **정책 동기화(Policy Sync)**  
   - 분산 학습 시, PPO 업데이트 후 에이전트들끼리 파라미터를 주기적으로 평균화(예: 협력 학습)  
   - 경쟁 상황에서는 평균화 대신, 개별 정책을 독립 유지.

4. **로깅 및 시각화**  
   - 다중 에이전트일수록 에이전트별 reward curve, 행동 패턴, 포지션 등을 분리해서 시각화하는 게 중요.  
   - `evaluation.py`에서 에이전트별로 성능 지표(Sharpe Ratio, Win Rate 등)를 측정.

---

## 5. 이 구조에서의 장점

1. **확장 용이**  
   - 새로운 전략(예: `risk_parity_ppo_agent.py`)을 간단히 추가하고, `multi_agent_manager.py`에서 등록만 해주면 됨.  
   - 환경(`multi_agent_env.py`) 바뀌어도 에이전트 로직은 크게 손대지 않아도 됨.

2. **유지보수 편의**  
   - 단일 에이전트 전략은 `single/`, 다중 에이전트 전략은 `multi/` 구역으로 명확히 분리.  
   - 체크포인트(`models/multi_agent/`) 또한 한 폴더에 모아서 관리 가능.

3. **공통 코드 재사용**  
   - PPO 업데이트 로직, 네트워크 아키텍처 등은 `agents/models/architectures/`나 에이전트 기반 클래스에서 재사용.  
   - 특정 지표나 데이터 전처리는 `training/utils/` 쪽에서 공유 가능.

4. **큰 그림 유지**  
   - `train_multi.py`로 다중 에이전트를 학습한다는 흐름이 명확해서 협업 시 이해가 쉬움.

---

## 6. 다음 단계(실전 적용) 체크리스트

1. **multi_agent_env.py** 최종 확인  
   - 에이전트별 state, action을 dict 형태로 반환/수신하도록 확실히 정리.  
   - 상호작용 규칙(협력/경쟁) 및 시장 메커니즘 구현.

2. **multi_agent_manager.py** 초기 버전 작성  
   - 에이전트 등록 로직, `act()`, `train_step()` 등 최소 기능 구현.  
   - Momentum, Mean-reversion, Market-making 등 간단한 PPO 기반 전략부터 시도.

3. **train_multi.py**를 통한 기본 학습 테스트  
   - 작은 에피소드 수로 환경과 에이전트 간 버그 체크.  
   - 로그(MLflow, TensorBoard 등)를 통해 reward curve, loss, 학습 안정성 확인.

4. **Metrics & Logging**  
   - 다중 에이전트 성능(개별 에이전트 보상, 총 포트폴리오 수익 등)을 저장하고 시각화.  
   - 경쟁 vs 협력 시나리오별 결과 비교.

5. **CI/CD & Testing**  
   - 유닛 테스트: agent별 act(), update() 메서드가 에러 없이 동작하는지  
   - 통합 테스트: `multi_agent_env.py` + `multi_agent_manager.py` + `train_multi.py` 시 에러 없이 에피소드가 정상 종료되는지  

---

## 결론

위에서 제안한 **디렉토리 구조**와 **파일별 역할**을 따르면,  
- 기존에 잘 만들어진 단일 에이전트 환경과 구분해서, 다중 에이전트 환경과 전략을 깔끔하게 확장**(확장성↑)**  
- 전략별 파일이 분리되어 **유지보수**와 **협업**이 쉬워지고  
- 협력/경쟁, 경험 공유 등 **멀티 에이전트 특유의 로직**을 `multi_agent_manager.py` 등 중앙 클래스에서 간단히 관리할 수 있어.  

**추가/개선**해야 할 부분은 크게 두 가지:

1. **`agents/strategies/multi/` 내부 구현**:  
   - Momentum, Mean-reversion, MarketMaker 등 다양한 PPO 에이전트  
   - `multi_agent_manager.py`에서 이것들을 하나로 묶어 관리

2. **`train_multi.py`에서 다중 에이전트 학습 루프**:  
   - 여러 에이전트의 action을 받아서 `env.step()`에 전달  
   - 받은 rewards/next_obs로 `manager.train_step()` 호출  
   - 주기적으로 모델 체크포인트 저장, 로그 기록

이렇게 하면 **다중 에이전트 시스템**으로 발전하는 데 필요한 뼈대가 완성될 거야! 이후에는 경험 공유, 메타 에이전트, 협력/경쟁 시나리오 등 고급 기능을 단계적으로 추가하면 된다. 화이팅! (๑˃̵ᴗ˂̵)و  

지금 이게 이번의 목표야! 현재까지 내가 알기로는 multi_agent_manager.py, 개별 에이전트 (momentum ppo.py), multi_agent_env.py는 구현이 되어 있고, 개별 에이전트를 조금 더 추가해 보고 싶어. 일단은 한 개 정도만 더 추가해 보고 어떻게 이게 작은 스케일로 잘 돌아가는지를 보고 싶어. 항상 하고 난 뒤에는 직접 pytest  (전체

```
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, Tuple
import logging
from agents.strategies.single.ppo_agent import PPOAgent
from gymnasium import spaces

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class MeanReversionPPOAgent(PPOAgent):
    """
    Mean Reversion strategy PPO agent that specializes in trading when assets deviate from their mean.
    Inherits from base PPO agent but adds mean reversion specific features and logic.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Initialize Mean Reversion PPO Agent.
        
        Args:
            config: Configuration dictionary containing:
                - All PPO parameters (learning_rate, gamma, etc.)
                - rsi_window: Window size for RSI calculation
                - bb_window: Window size for Bollinger Bands
                - bb_std: Number of standard deviations for Bollinger Bands
                - oversold_threshold: RSI threshold for oversold condition
                - overbought_threshold: RSI threshold for overbought condition
        """
        # Calculate augmented observation space
        base_obs_space = config["observation_space"]
        n_reversion_features = 3  # RSI, BB_upper_dist, BB_lower_dist
        
        if isinstance(base_obs_space, spaces.Box):
            if len(base_obs_space.shape) == 2:  # (window_size, features)
                total_features = base_obs_space.shape[0] * base_obs_space.shape[1] + n_reversion_features
                
                flat_obs_space = spaces.Box(
                    low=-np.inf,
                    high=np.inf,
                    shape=(total_features,),
                    dtype=np.float32
                )
            else:
                raise ValueError("Observation space must be 2D (window_size, features)")
            
            self.original_obs_space = base_obs_space
            self.n_reversion_features = n_reversion_features
        else:
            raise ValueError("Observation space must be Box")
        
        # Initialize base PPO agent with flattened observation space
        super().__init__(
            observation_space=flat_obs_space,
            action_space=config["action_space"],
            learning_rate=config.get("learning_rate", 3e-4),
            gamma=config.get("gamma", 0.99),
            gae_lambda=config.get("gae_lambda", 0.95),
            clip_epsilon=config.get("clip_epsilon", 0.2),
            c1=config.get("c1", 1.0),
            c2=config.get("c2", 0.01),
            c3=config.get("c3", 0.5),
            batch_size=config.get("batch_size", 64),
            n_epochs=config.get("n_epochs", 10),
            target_kl=config.get("target_kl", 0.015),
            device=config.get("device", "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
        )
        
        # Mean reversion specific parameters
        self.rsi_window = config.get("rsi_window", 14)
        self.bb_window = config.get("bb_window", 20)
        self.bb_std = config.get("bb_std", 2.0)
        self.oversold_threshold = config.get("oversold_threshold", 30)
        self.overbought_threshold = config.get("overbought_threshold", 70)
        
        logger.info(
            f"Initialized MeanReversionPPOAgent with RSI window={self.rsi_window}, "
            f"BB window={self.bb_window}, BB std={self.bb_std}"
        )
    
    def _calculate_rsi(self, prices: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """Calculate RSI for a price series."""
        deltas = np.diff(prices)
        gain = np.where(deltas > 0, deltas, 0)
        loss = np.where(deltas < 0, -deltas, 0)
        
        avg_gain = np.mean(gain)
        avg_loss = np.mean(loss)
        
        if avg_loss == 0:
            return 100.0
        
        rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
        return 100 - (100 / (1 + rs))
    
    def _calculate_bollinger_bands(self, prices: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """Calculate Bollinger Bands for a price series."""
        mean = np.mean(prices)
        std = np.std(prices)
        upper = mean + self.bb_std * std
        lower = mean - self.bb_std * std
        return upper, lower
    
    def _calculate_reversion_features(self, state: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Calculate mean reversion specific features from the state.
        
        Args:
            state: Raw state observation
            
        Returns:
            Mean reversion features as numpy array
        """
        if len(state.shape) == 1:
            state = state.reshape(1, -1)
        elif len(state.shape) == 3:
            state = state[:, -self.bb_window:, :]
            
        if state.shape[-1] >= 4:  # Ensure we have enough features
            close_prices = state[..., 3]  # Get close prices
            
            if len(close_prices.shape) == 2:
                # Batch processing
                rsi = np.array([
                    self._calculate_rsi(prices[-self.rsi_window:])
                    for prices in close_prices
                ])
                
                bb_features = np.array([
                    self._calculate_bollinger_bands(prices[-self.bb_window:])
                    for prices in close_prices
                ])
                bb_upper = bb_features[:, 0]
                bb_lower = bb_features[:, 1]
                
                current_prices = close_prices[:, -1]
                bb_upper_dist = (bb_upper - current_prices) / current_prices
                bb_lower_dist = (current_prices - bb_lower) / current_prices
                
            else:
                # Single sample processing
                rsi = self._calculate_rsi(close_prices[-self.rsi_window:])
                bb_upper, bb_lower = self._calculate_bollinger_bands(close_prices[-self.bb_window:])
                current_price = close_prices[-1]
                bb_upper_dist = (bb_upper - current_price) / current_price
                bb_lower_dist = (current_price - bb_lower) / current_price
            
            return np.column_stack([rsi, bb_upper_dist, bb_lower_dist]) if len(state.shape) > 2 else np.array([rsi, bb_upper_dist, bb_lower_dist])
        else:
            shape = (state.shape[0], 3) if len(state.shape) > 2 else (3,)
            return np.zeros(shape, dtype=np.float32)
    
    def get_action(self, state: np.ndarray, deterministic: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Get action from policy network with mean reversion considerations.
        
        Args:
            state: Current state observation
            deterministic: Whether to use deterministic action selection
            
        Returns:
            Selected action as numpy array
        """
        reversion_features = self._calculate_reversion_features(state)
        
        if len(state.shape) == 3:  # (batch, window, features)
            batch_size = state.shape[0]
            flat_state = state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, reversion_features], axis=1)
        else:  # (window, features)
            flat_state = state.reshape(1, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, reversion_features.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
        
        action = super().get_action(augmented_state.reshape(-1), deterministic)
        
        # Apply mean reversion based action modification
        if len(reversion_features.shape) > 1:
            rsi = reversion_features[:, 0]
            bb_upper_dist = reversion_features[:, 1]
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[:, 2]
            
            # Modify actions based on RSI and Bollinger Bands
            action = np.where(
                (rsi < self.oversold_threshold) & (bb_lower_dist < 0.01),
                np.maximum(action, 0),  # Strong buy signal
                np.where(
                    (rsi > self.overbought_threshold) & (bb_upper_dist < 0.01),
                    np.minimum(action, 0),  # Strong sell signal
                    action
                )
            )
        else:
            rsi = reversion_features[0]
            bb_upper_dist = reversion_features[1]
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[2]
            
            if rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.01:
                action = np.maximum(action, 0)  # Strong buy signal
            elif rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.01:
                action = np.minimum(action, 0)  # Strong sell signal
            
        return action
    
    def train_step(self, state: np.ndarray, action: np.ndarray, 
                  reward: float, next_state: np.ndarray, 
                  done: bool) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Train the agent on a single state transition with mean reversion considerations.
        
        Args:
            state: Current state
            action: Action taken
            reward: Reward received
            next_state: Next state
            done: Whether episode is done
            
        Returns:
            Dictionary of training metrics
        """
        state_reversion = self._calculate_reversion_features(state)
        next_state_reversion = self._calculate_reversion_features(next_state)
        
        if len(state.shape) == 3:  # (batch, window, features)
            batch_size = state.shape[0]
            flat_state = state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            flat_next_state = next_state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, state_reversion], axis=1)
            augmented_next_state = np.concatenate([flat_next_state, next_state_reversion], axis=1)
        else:  # (window, features)
            flat_state = state.reshape(1, -1)
            flat_next_state = next_state.reshape(1, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, state_reversion.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
            augmented_next_state = np.concatenate([flat_next_state, next_state_reversion.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
        
        # Add mean reversion based reward modification
        reversion_reward = 0.0
        if len(state_reversion.shape) > 1:
            rsi = state_reversion[:, 0]
            bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[:, 1]
            bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[:, 2]
            
            reversion_reward = np.where(
                ((rsi < self.oversold_threshold) & (bb_lower_dist < 0.01) & (action > 0)) |
                ((rsi > self.overbought_threshold) & (bb_upper_dist < 0.01) & (action < 0)),
                0.1, 0.0
            )
        else:
            rsi = state_reversion[0]
            bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[1]
            bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[2]
            
            if (rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.01 and action > 0) or \
               (rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.01 and action < 0):
                reversion_reward = 0.1  # Reward for following mean reversion signals
            
        modified_reward = reward + reversion_reward
        
        metrics = super().train_step(
            augmented_state.reshape(-1), action, modified_reward, augmented_next_state.reshape(-1), done
        )
        
        if metrics is None:
            return {
                "reversion_reward": 0.0,
                "rsi_value": 0.0,
                "bb_upper_dist": 0.0,
                "bb_lower_dist": 0.0
            }
        
        if len(state_reversion.shape) > 1:
            metrics.update({
                "reversion_reward": float(np.mean(reversion_reward)),
                "rsi_value": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 0])),
                "bb_upper_dist": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 1])),
                "bb_lower_dist": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 2]))
            })
        else:
            metrics.update({
                "reversion_reward": float(reversion_reward),
                "rsi_value": float(state_reversion[0]),
                "bb_upper_dist": float(state_reversion[1]),
                "bb_lower_dist": float(state_reversion[2])
            })
        
        return metrics
    
    def learn_from_shared_experience(self, shared_buffer: list) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Learn from shared experience buffer with mean reversion strategy focus.
        
        Args:
            shared_buffer: List of experience tuples from other agents
            
        Returns:
            Dictionary of training metrics
        """
        # Filter for experiences that align with mean reversion strategy
        filtered_buffer = []
        for exp in shared_buffer:
            state, action, reward, next_state, done = exp
            state_reversion = self._calculate_reversion_features(state)
            
            if len(state_reversion.shape) > 1:
                rsi = state_reversion[:, 0]
                bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[:, 1]
                bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[:, 2]
                
                # Only learn from experiences that match our strategy
                if ((rsi < self.oversold_threshold).any() and (bb_lower_dist < 0.01).any()) or \
                   ((rsi > self.overbought_threshold).any() and (bb_upper_dist < 0.01).any()):
                    filtered_buffer.append(exp)
            else:
                rsi = state_reversion[0]
                bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[1]
                bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[2]
                
                if (rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.01) or \
                   (rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.01):
                    filtered_buffer.append(exp)
        
        # Learn from filtered experiences
        if filtered_buffer:
            return super().learn_from_shared_experience(filtered_buffer)
        else:
            return {}
```

```
                "reversion_reward": 0.0,
                "rsi_value": 0.0,
                "bb_upper_dist": 0.0,
                "bb_lower_dist": 0.0
            }
        
        if len(state_reversion.shape) > 1:
            metrics.update({
                "reversion_reward": float(np.mean(reversion_reward)),
                "rsi_value": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 0])),
                "bb_upper_dist": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 1])),
                "bb_lower_dist": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 2]))
            })
        else:
            metrics.update({
                "reversion_reward": float(reversion_reward),
                "rsi_value": float(state_reversion[0]),
                "bb_upper_dist": float(state_reversion[1]),
                "bb_lower_dist": float(state_reversion[2])
            })
        
        return metrics
    
    def learn_from_shared_experience(self, shared_buffer: list) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Learn from shared experience buffer with mean reversion strategy focus.
        
        Args:
            shared_buffer: List of experience tuples from other agents
            
        Returns:
            Dictionary of training metrics
        """
        # Filter for experiences that align with mean reversion strategy
        filtered_buffer = []
        for exp in shared_buffer:
            state, action, reward, next_state, done = exp
            state_reversion = self._calculate_reversion_features(state)
            
            if len(state_reversion.shape) > 1:
                rsi = state_reversion[:, 0]
                bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[:, 1]
                bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[:, 2]
                
                # Only learn from experiences that match our strategy
                if ((rsi < self.oversold_threshold).any() and (bb_lower_dist < 0.01).any()) or \
                   ((rsi > self.overbought_threshold).any() and (bb_upper_dist < 0.01).any()):
                    filtered_buffer.append(exp)
            else:
                rsi = state_reversion[0]
                bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[1]
                bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[2]
                
                if (rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.01) or \
                   (rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.01):
                    filtered_buffer.append(exp)
        
        # Learn from filtered experiences
        if filtered_buffer:
            return super().learn_from_shared_experience(filtered_buffer)
        else:
            return {}
```

이제 새로운 Mean Reversion PPO 에이전트를 구현했습니다. 이 에이전트는 다음과 같은 특징을 가지고 있습니다:

1. **RSI와 Bollinger Bands 기반 전략**:
   - RSI를 통해 과매수/과매도 상태를 판단
   - Bollinger Bands를 통해 가격의 평균 회귀 가능성을 평가

2. **특화된 상태 공간**:
   - 기존 상태에 RSI, BB 상단까지의 거리, BB 하단까지의 거리를 추가
   - 이를 통해 평균 회귀 시그널을 더 잘 포착

3. **전략적 행동 수정**:
   - RSI가 과매도(30 이하)이고 BB 하단에 가까울 때 매수 신호 강화
   - RSI가 과매수(70 이상)이고 BB 상단에 가까울 때 매도 신호 강화

4. **전략 특화 보상**:
   - 평균 회귀 시그널에 맞는 포지션을 취했을 때 추가 보상
   - 이를 통해 평균 회귀 전략에 맞는 행동을 더 강화

5. **선택적 경험 학습**:
   - 다른 에이전트의 경험 중 평균 회귀 패턴이 있는 경험만 선택적으로 학습
   - 이를 통해 전략의 특성을 유지하면서 협력 학습 가능

```
import numpy as np
import pytest
from gymnasium import spaces
from agents.strategies.multi.mean_reversion_ppo_agent import MeanReversionPPOAgent

@pytest.fixture
def sample_config():
    return {
        "observation_space": spaces.Box(
            low=-np.inf, high=np.inf,
            shape=(20, 5),  # window_size=20, features=5 (OHLCV)
            dtype=np.float32
        ),
        "action_space": spaces.Box(
            low=-1, high=1,
            shape=(1,),
            dtype=np.float32
        ),
        "rsi_window": 14,
        "bb_window": 20,
        "bb_std": 2.0,
        "oversold_threshold": 30,
        "overbought_threshold": 70
    }

def test_agent_initialization(sample_config):
    agent = MeanReversionPPOAgent(sample_config)
    assert agent.rsi_window == 14
    assert agent.bb_window == 20
    assert agent.bb_std == 2.0
    assert agent.oversold_threshold == 30
    assert agent.overbought_threshold == 70

def test_rsi_calculation():
    agent = MeanReversionPPOAgent(sample_config())
    
    # Test RSI with upward trend
    prices = np.array([10.0, 10.5, 11.0, 11.5, 12.0])
    rsi = agent._calculate_rsi(prices)
    assert rsi > 50  # RSI should be high in upward trend
    
    # Test RSI with downward trend
    prices = np.array([12.0, 11.5, 11.0, 10.5, 10.0])
    rsi = agent._calculate_rsi(prices)
    assert rsi < 50  # RSI should be low in downward trend

def test_bollinger_bands_calculation():
    agent = MeanReversionPPOAgent(sample_config())
    
    # Test with flat prices
    prices = np.array([10.0] * 20)
    upper, lower = agent._calculate_bollinger_bands(prices)
    assert upper == 10.0  # Upper band should equal price when no volatility
    assert lower == 10.0  # Lower band should equal price when no volatility
    
    # Test with volatile prices
    prices = np.array([10.0 + i for i in range(20)])
    upper, lower = agent._calculate_bollinger_bands(prices)
    assert upper > np.mean(prices)  # Upper band should be above mean
    assert lower < np.mean(prices)  # Lower band should be below mean

def test_get_action_mean_reversion():
    agent = MeanReversionPPOAgent(sample_config())
    
    # Create a state where price is at BB upper and RSI is high (overbought)
    state = np.zeros((20, 5))
    state[:, 3] = 100.0  # Set close prices
    state[-5:, 3] = 120.0  # Last 5 prices higher to create overbought
    
    action = agent.get_action(state)
    assert action <= 0  # Should prefer selling in overbought condition
    
    # Create a state where price is at BB lower and RSI is low (oversold)
    state = np.zeros((20, 5))
    state[:, 3] = 100.0  # Set close prices
    state[-5:, 3] = 80.0  # Last 5 prices lower to create oversold
    
    action = agent.get_action(state)
    assert action >= 0  # Should prefer buying in oversold condition

def test_train_step_reward_modification():
    agent = MeanReversionPPOAgent(sample_config())
    
    # Create oversold state
    state = np.zeros((20, 5))
    state[:, 3] = 100.0
    state[-5:, 3] = 80.0  # Create oversold condition
    
    next_state = state.copy()
    next_state[-1, 3] = 85.0  # Price bounced back
    
    # Test reward modification for correct mean reversion trade
    metrics = agent.train_step(
        state=state,
        action=np.array([1.0]),  # Buy in oversold
        reward=0.1,
        next_state=next_state,
        done=False
    )
    
    assert metrics["reversion_reward"] > 0  # Should get extra reward for correct mean reversion trade
    assert "rsi_value" in metrics
    assert "bb_upper_dist" in metrics
    assert "bb_lower_dist" in metrics
```

```
// ... existing code ...
    def get_action(self, state: np.ndarray, deterministic: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Get action from policy network with mean reversion considerations.
        
        Args:
            state: Current state observation
            deterministic: Whether to use deterministic action selection
            
        Returns:
            Selected action as numpy array
        """
        reversion_features = self._calculate_reversion_features(state)
        
        if len(state.shape) == 3:  # (batch, window, features)
            batch_size = state.shape[0]
            flat_state = state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, reversion_features], axis=1)
        else:  # (window, features)
            flat_state = state.reshape(1, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, reversion_features.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
        
        action = super().get_action(augmented_state.reshape(-1), deterministic)
        
        # Apply mean reversion based action modification
        if len(reversion_features.shape) > 1:
            rsi = reversion_features[:, 0]
            bb_upper_dist = reversion_features[:, 1]
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[:, 2]
            
            # Modify actions based on RSI and Bollinger Bands
            # For oversold conditions (RSI < 30 and price near lower band)
            oversold_mask = (rsi < self.oversold_threshold) & (bb_lower_dist < 0.02)
            # For overbought conditions (RSI > 70 and price near upper band)
            overbought_mask = (rsi > self.overbought_threshold) & (bb_upper_dist < 0.02)
            
            # Apply stronger action modifications
            action = np.where(
                oversold_mask,
                np.maximum(action, 0.3),  # Stronger buy signal
                np.where(
                    overbought_mask,
                    np.minimum(action, -0.3),  # Stronger sell signal
                    action
                )
            )
        else:
            rsi = reversion_features[0]
            bb_upper_dist = reversion_features[1]
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[2]
            
            if rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.02:
                action = np.maximum(action, 0.3)  # Stronger buy signal
            elif rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.02:
                action = np.minimum(action, -0.3)  # Stronger sell signal
            
        return action
    
    def train_step(self, state: np.ndarray, action: np.ndarray, 
                  reward: float, next_state: np.ndarray, 
                  done: bool) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Train the agent on a single state transition with mean reversion considerations.
        
        Args:
            state: Current state
            action: Action taken
            reward: Reward received
            next_state: Next state
            done: Whether episode is done
            
        Returns:
            Dictionary of training metrics
        """
        state_reversion = self._calculate_reversion_features(state)
        next_state_reversion = self._calculate_reversion_features(next_state)
        
        if len(state.shape) == 3:  # (batch, window, features)
            batch_size = state.shape[0]
            flat_state = state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            flat_next_state = next_state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, state_reversion], axis=1)
            augmented_next_state = np.concatenate([flat_next_state, next_state_reversion], axis=1)
        else:  # (window, features)
            flat_state = state.reshape(1, -1)
            flat_next_state = next_state.reshape(1, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, state_reversion.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
            augmented_next_state = np.concatenate([flat_next_state, next_state_reversion.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
        
        # Add mean reversion based reward modification
        reversion_reward = 0.0
        if len(state_reversion.shape) > 1:
            rsi = state_reversion[:, 0]
            bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[:, 1]
            bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[:, 2]
            
            # Calculate reward for mean reversion signals
            oversold_mask = (rsi < self.oversold_threshold) & (bb_lower_dist < 0.02)
            overbought_mask = (rsi > self.overbought_threshold) & (bb_upper_dist < 0.02)
            
            reversion_reward = np.where(
                (oversold_mask & (action > 0.2)) |  # Strong buy in oversold
                (overbought_mask & (action < -0.2)),  # Strong sell in overbought
                0.2,  # Larger reward for following strong signals
                np.where(
                    (oversold_mask & (action > 0)) |  # Any buy in oversold
                    (overbought_mask & (action < 0)),  # Any sell in overbought
                    0.1,  # Smaller reward for following signals
                    0.0
                )
            )
        else:
            rsi = state_reversion[0]
            bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[1]
            bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[2]
            
            # Strong mean reversion signals
            if ((rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.02 and action > 0.2) or
                (rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.02 and action < -0.2)):
                reversion_reward = 0.2  # Larger reward for strong signals
            # Weaker mean reversion signals
            elif ((rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.02 and action > 0) or
                  (rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.02 and action < 0)):
                reversion_reward = 0.1  # Smaller reward for following signals
            
        modified_reward = reward + reversion_reward
// ... existing code ...
```

```
// ... existing code ...
    def _calculate_rsi(self, prices: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """Calculate RSI for a price series."""
        if len(prices) < 2:
            return 50.0  # Return neutral RSI for insufficient data
            
        deltas = np.diff(prices)
        seed = deltas[-self.rsi_window:]  # Use the last window_size changes
        
        # Separate gains and losses
        gains = seed.copy()
        losses = seed.copy()
        gains[gains < 0] = 0
        losses[losses > 0] = 0
        losses = -losses  # Make losses positive
        
        # Calculate average gains and losses
        avg_gain = np.mean(gains) if len(gains) > 0 else 0
        avg_loss = np.mean(losses) if len(losses) > 0 else 0
        
        if avg_loss == 0:
            return 100.0 if avg_gain > 0 else 50.0
        
        rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
        rsi = 100 - (100 / (1 + rs))
        
        return float(rsi)
    
    def _calculate_bollinger_bands(self, prices: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """Calculate Bollinger Bands for a price series."""
        if len(prices) < self.bb_window:
            return prices[-1], prices[-1]  # Return current price as both bands if insufficient data
            
        window_prices = prices[-self.bb_window:]  # Use the last window_size prices
        mean = np.mean(window_prices)
        std = np.std(window_prices)
        
        upper = mean + self.bb_std * std
        lower = mean - self.bb_std * std
        
        # Ensure bands don't cross
        upper = max(upper, mean)
        lower = min(lower, mean)
        
        return upper, lower
// ... existing code ...
```

```
// ... existing code ...
    def _calculate_rsi(self, prices: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        Calculate RSI using exponential moving averages.
        
        Args:
            prices: Array of price values
            
        Returns:
            RSI value between 0 and 100
        """
        if len(prices) < 2:
            return 50.0  # Return neutral RSI for insufficient data
        
        # Calculate price changes
        deltas = np.diff(prices)
        
        # Separate gains and losses
        gains = np.zeros_like(deltas)
        losses = np.zeros_like(deltas)
        
        gains[deltas > 0] = deltas[deltas > 0]
        losses[deltas < 0] = -deltas[deltas < 0]
        
        # Calculate exponential moving averages
        alpha = 2.0 / (self.rsi_window + 1)
        
        gains_ema = np.zeros_like(gains)
        losses_ema = np.zeros_like(losses)
        
        # Initialize first value
        gains_ema[0] = gains[0]
        losses_ema[0] = losses[0]
        
        # Calculate EMAs
        for i in range(1, len(gains)):
            gains_ema[i] = alpha * gains[i] + (1 - alpha) * gains_ema[i-1]
            losses_ema[i] = alpha * losses[i] + (1 - alpha) * losses_ema[i-1]
        
        # Get the most recent values
        avg_gain = gains_ema[-1]
        avg_loss = losses_ema[-1]
        
        # Calculate RSI
        if avg_loss == 0:
            return 100.0 if avg_gain > 0 else 50.0
        
        rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
        rsi = 100.0 - (100.0 / (1.0 + rs))
        
        return float(rsi)
// ... existing code ...
```

```
// ... existing code ...
    def get_action(self, state: np.ndarray, deterministic: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Get action from policy network with mean reversion considerations.
        
        Args:
            state: Current state observation
            deterministic: Whether to use deterministic action selection
            
        Returns:
            Selected action as numpy array
        """
        reversion_features = self._calculate_reversion_features(state)
        
        if len(state.shape) == 3:  # (batch, window, features)
            batch_size = state.shape[0]
            flat_state = state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, reversion_features], axis=1)
        else:  # (window, features)
            flat_state = state.reshape(1, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, reversion_features.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
        
        # Get base action from policy network
        base_action = super().get_action(augmented_state.reshape(-1), deterministic)
        
        # Apply mean reversion based action modification
        if len(reversion_features.shape) > 1:
            rsi = reversion_features[:, 0]
            bb_upper_dist = reversion_features[:, 1]
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[:, 2]
            
            # Strong oversold condition
            oversold_mask = (rsi < self.oversold_threshold) & (bb_lower_dist < 0.02)
            # Strong overbought condition
            overbought_mask = (rsi > self.overbought_threshold) & (bb_upper_dist < 0.02)
            
            # Apply action modifications with stronger bias
            action = np.where(
                oversold_mask,
                np.maximum(base_action + 0.5, 0.3),  # Strong buy bias
                np.where(
                    overbought_mask,
                    np.minimum(base_action - 0.5, -0.3),  # Strong sell bias
                    base_action
                )
            )
        else:
            rsi = reversion_features[0]
            bb_upper_dist = reversion_features[1]
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[2]
            
            # Strong oversold condition
            if rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.02:
                action = np.maximum(base_action + 0.5, 0.3)  # Strong buy bias
            # Strong overbought condition
            elif rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.02:
                action = np.minimum(base_action - 0.5, -0.3)  # Strong sell bias
            else:
                action = base_action
            
        return action
```

```
    def train_step(self, state: np.ndarray, action: np.ndarray, 
                  reward: float, next_state: np.ndarray, 
                  done: bool) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Train the agent on a single state transition with mean reversion considerations.
        
        Args:
            state: Current state
            action: Action taken
            reward: Reward received
            next_state: Next state
            done: Whether episode is done
            
        Returns:
            Dictionary of training metrics
        """
        state_reversion = self._calculate_reversion_features(state)
        next_state_reversion = self._calculate_reversion_features(next_state)
        
        if len(state.shape) == 3:  # (batch, window, features)
            batch_size = state.shape[0]
            flat_state = state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            flat_next_state = next_state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, state_reversion], axis=1)
            augmented_next_state = np.concatenate([flat_next_state, next_state_reversion], axis=1)
        else:  # (window, features)
            flat_state = state.reshape(1, -1)
            flat_next_state = next_state.reshape(1, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, state_reversion.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
            augmented_next_state = np.concatenate([flat_next_state, next_state_reversion.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
        
        # Add mean reversion based reward modification
        reversion_reward = 0.0
        if len(state_reversion.shape) > 1:
            rsi = state_reversion[:, 0]
            bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[:, 1]
            bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[:, 2]
            
            # Calculate reward for mean reversion signals
            oversold_mask = (rsi < self.oversold_threshold) & (bb_lower_dist < 0.02)
            overbought_mask = (rsi > self.overbought_threshold) & (bb_upper_dist < 0.02)
            
            # Calculate price movement
            current_prices = state[..., 3]
            next_prices = next_state[..., 3]
            price_changes = (next_prices - current_prices) / current_prices
            
            # Reward matrix for different scenarios
            reversion_reward = np.where(
                # Strong mean reversion signals with correct actions and profitable outcomes
                ((oversold_mask & (action > 0.2) & (price_changes > 0)) |
                 (overbought_mask & (action < -0.2) & (price_changes < 0))),
                0.5,  # Large reward for strong signals with profit
                np.where(
                    # Weaker mean reversion signals with correct actions
                    ((oversold_mask & (action > 0)) |
                     (overbought_mask & (action < 0))),
                    0.2,  # Medium reward for following signals
                    0.0
                )
            )
        else:
            rsi = state_reversion[0]
            bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[1]
            bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[2]
            
            # Calculate price movement
            current_price = state[..., 3][-1]
            next_price = next_state[..., 3][-1]
            price_change = (next_price - current_price) / current_price
            
            # Strong mean reversion signals with correct actions and profitable outcomes
            if ((rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.02 and action > 0.2 and price_change > 0) or
                (rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.02 and action < -0.2 and price_change < 0)):
                reversion_reward = 0.5  # Large reward for strong signals with profit
            # Weaker mean reversion signals with correct actions
            elif ((rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.02 and action > 0) or
                  (rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.02 and action < 0)):
                reversion_reward = 0.2  # Medium reward for following signals
            
        modified_reward = reward + reversion_reward
        
        metrics = super().train_step(
            augmented_state.reshape(-1), action, modified_reward, augmented_next_state.reshape(-1), done
        )
        
        if metrics is None:
            return {
                "reversion_reward": 0.0,
                "rsi_value": 0.0,
                "bb_upper_dist": 0.0,
                "bb_lower_dist": 0.0
            }
        
        if len(state_reversion.shape) > 1:
            metrics.update({
                "reversion_reward": float(np.mean(reversion_reward)),
                "rsi_value": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 0])),
                "bb_upper_dist": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 1])),
                "bb_lower_dist": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 2]))
            })
        else:
            metrics.update({
                "reversion_reward": float(reversion_reward),
                "rsi_value": float(state_reversion[0]),
                "bb_upper_dist": float(state_reversion[1]),
                "bb_lower_dist": float(state_reversion[2])
            })
        
        return metrics
```

```
def test_train_step_reward_modification(sample_config):
    agent = MeanReversionPPOAgent(sample_config)
    
    # Create a price series with initial stability followed by decline
    state = np.zeros((20, 5))
    
    # First set stable prices with slight upward trend
    for i in range(15):
        state[i, 3] = 100.0 * (1.01 ** i)  # Each step up 1%
    
    # Then create a sharp decline to trigger oversold
    for i in range(5):
        state[15+i, 3] = state[14, 3] * (0.95 ** (i+1))  # Each step down 5%
    
    # Ensure OHLC values are consistent
    for i in range(20):
        state[i, 0] = state[i, 3] * 1.02  # High above close
        state[i, 1] = state[i, 3] * 0.98  # Low below close
        state[i, 2] = state[i, 3]  # Open same as close for simplicity
        state[i, 4] = 1000000  # Constant volume
    
    next_state = state.copy()
    # Price bounces back significantly (mean reversion)
    next_state[-1, 3] *= 1.05  # 5% bounce
    next_state[-1, 0] = next_state[-1, 3] * 1.02
    next_state[-1, 1] = next_state[-1, 3] * 0.98
    next_state[-1, 2] = next_state[-1, 3]
    
    # Print initial debug information
    features = agent._calculate_reversion_features(state)
    print(f"Initial RSI: {features[0]}")
    print(f"Initial BB Upper Distance: {features[1]}")
    print(f"Initial BB Lower Distance: {features[2]}")
    print(f"Price series: {state[:, 3]}")
    print(f"Last 5 price changes (%): {[(state[i+1, 3] / state[i, 3] - 1) * 100 for i in range(-6, -1)]}")
    
    # Test reward modification for strong mean reversion trade
    metrics = agent.train_step(
        state=state,
        action=np.array([0.5]),  # Strong buy in oversold (> 0.2 threshold)
        reward=0.1,
        next_state=next_state,
        done=False
    )
    
    # Print final debug information
    print(f"Final RSI: {metrics['rsi_value']}")
    print(f"Final BB Lower Distance: {metrics['bb_lower_dist']}")
    print(f"Final Reversion Reward: {metrics['reversion_reward']}")
    print(f"Price bounce: {(next_state[-1, 3] / state[-1, 3] - 1) * 100:.2f}%")
    
    assert metrics["reversion_reward"] > 0  # Should get extra reward for correct mean reversion trade
    assert metrics["rsi_value"] < 30  # RSI should be in oversold territory
    assert metrics["bb_lower_dist"] < 0.02  # Price should be near BB lower
    assert "bb_upper_dist" in metrics
```

```
// ... existing code ...
    def _calculate_rsi(self, prices: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        Calculate RSI using Wilder's smoothing technique.
        
        Args:
            prices: Array of price values
            
        Returns:
            RSI value between 0 and 100
        """
        if len(prices) < self.rsi_window + 1:
            return 50.0  # Return neutral RSI for insufficient data
        
        # Calculate price changes
        deltas = np.diff(prices)
        
        # Separate gains and losses
        gains = np.zeros_like(deltas)
        losses = np.zeros_like(deltas)
        
        gains[deltas > 0] = deltas[deltas > 0]
        losses[deltas < 0] = -deltas[deltas < 0]
        
        # Calculate initial averages
        avg_gain = np.mean(gains[:self.rsi_window])
        avg_loss = np.mean(losses[:self.rsi_window])
        
        # Calculate smoothed averages
        for i in range(self.rsi_window, len(gains)):
            avg_gain = (avg_gain * (self.rsi_window - 1) + gains[i]) / self.rsi_window
            avg_loss = (avg_loss * (self.rsi_window - 1) + losses[i]) / self.rsi_window
        
        # Calculate final RSI
        if avg_loss == 0:
            return 100.0 if avg_gain > 0 else 50.0
        
        rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
        rsi = 100.0 - (100.0 / (1.0 + rs))
        
        # Ensure RSI stays within bounds
        rsi = max(0.0, min(100.0, rsi))
        
        return float(rsi)
// ... existing code ...
```

```
// ... existing code ...
    def get_action(self, state: np.ndarray, deterministic: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Get action from policy network with mean reversion considerations.
        
        Args:
            state: Current state observation
            deterministic: Whether to use deterministic action selection
            
        Returns:
            Selected action as numpy array
        """
        reversion_features = self._calculate_reversion_features(state)
        
        if len(state.shape) == 3:  # (batch, window, features)
            batch_size = state.shape[0]
            flat_state = state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, reversion_features], axis=1)
        else:  # (window, features)
            flat_state = state.reshape(1, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, reversion_features.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
        
        # Get base action from policy network
        base_action = super().get_action(augmented_state.reshape(-1), deterministic)
        
        # Apply mean reversion based action modification
        if len(reversion_features.shape) > 1:
            rsi = reversion_features[:, 0]
            bb_upper_dist = reversion_features[:, 1]
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[:, 2]
            
            # Calculate mean reversion signals
            oversold_signal = (rsi < self.oversold_threshold) & (bb_lower_dist < 0.02)
            overbought_signal = (rsi > self.overbought_threshold) & (bb_upper_dist < 0.02)
            
            # Calculate action bias based on signals
            action_bias = np.zeros_like(base_action)
            action_bias[oversold_signal] = 1.0  # Strong buy bias
            action_bias[overbought_signal] = -1.0  # Strong sell bias
            
            # Blend base action with bias
            action = 0.7 * action_bias + 0.3 * base_action
            
            # Ensure action stays within bounds
            action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0)
        else:
            rsi = reversion_features[0]
            bb_upper_dist = reversion_features[1]
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[2]
            
            # Calculate action bias based on signals
            if rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.02:
                action_bias = 1.0  # Strong buy bias
            elif rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.02:
                action_bias = -1.0  # Strong sell bias
            else:
                action_bias = 0.0
            
            # Blend base action with bias
            action = 0.7 * action_bias + 0.3 * base_action[0]
            
            # Ensure action stays within bounds and wrap in array
            action = np.array([np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0)])
            
        return action
```

```
        """

        Args:
            state: Current state
            action: Action taken
            reward: Reward received
            next_state: Next state
            done: Whether episode is done

        next_state_reversion = self._calculate_reversion_features(next_state)

            flat_state = state.reshape(batch_size, -1)

            augmented_next_state = np.concatenate([flat_next_state, next_state_reversion], axis=1)
        else:  # (window, features)

            flat_next_state = next_state.reshape(1, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, state_reversion.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
            augmented_next_state = np.concatenate([flat_next_state, next_state_reversion.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
        
        # Add mean reversion based reward modification
        reversion_reward = 0.0
        if len(state_reversion.shape) > 1:
            rsi = state_reversion[:, 0]
            bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[:, 1]
            bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[:, 2]
            
            # Calculate price movement
            current_prices = state[..., 3]
            next_prices = next_state[..., 3]
            price_changes = (next_prices - current_prices) / current_prices
            
            # Calculate mean reversion signals

            overbought_signal = (rsi > self.overbought_threshold) & (bb_upper_dist < 0.02)
            
            # Calculate rewards for different scenarios
            buy_reward = np.where(oversold_signal & (action > 0.2) & (price_changes > 0), 
                                0.5 * np.abs(price_changes), 0.0)
            sell_reward = np.where(overbought_signal & (action < -0.2) & (price_changes < 0),
                                 0.5 * np.abs(price_changes), 0.0)
            
            # Combine rewards
            reversion_reward = buy_reward + sell_reward

            direction_reward = np.where(
                (oversold_signal & (action > 0)) | (overbought_signal & (action < 0)),

                elif action < 0:
                    reversion_reward = 0.1  # Small reward for correct direction

        )
        
        if metrics is None:
```

```
        state[i, 1] = state[i, 3] - daily_volatility  # Low
```

```
def test_train_step_reward_modification(sample_config):

    # Ensure OHLC values are consistent and add some volatility
    for i in range(20):
        daily_volatility = state[i, 3] * 0.02  # 2% daily volatility
        state[i, 0] = state[i, 3] + daily_volatility  # High
        state[i, 1] = state[i, 3] - daily_volatility  # Low
        state[i, 2] = state[i, 3]  # Open same as close for simplicity
        state[i, 4] = 1000000  # Constant volume
    
    next_state = state.copy()
    # Price bounces back significantly (mean reversion)
    bounce_pct = 0.10  # 10% bounce
    next_state[-1, 3] *= (1.0 + bounce_pct)
    next_state[-1, 0] = next_state[-1, 3] * 1.02  # High
    next_state[-1, 1] = next_state[-1, 3] * 0.98  # Low
    next_state[-1, 2] = next_state[-1, 3]  # Open
    
    # Print initial debug information
    features = agent._calculate_reversion_features(state)
    print(f"Initial RSI: {features[0]}")
    print(f"Initial BB Upper Distance: {features[1]}")
    print(f"Initial BB Lower Distance: {features[2]}")
    print(f"Price series: {state[:, 3]}")
    print(f"Last 5 price changes (%): {[(state[i+1, 3] / state[i, 3] - 1) * 100 for i in range(-6, -1)]}")
    
    # Test reward modification for strong mean reversion trade
    metrics = agent.train_step(
        state=state,
        action=np.array([0.8]),  # Very strong buy in oversold (> 0.2 threshold)
        reward=0.1,
        next_state=next_state,
        done=False
    )
    
    # Print final debug information
    print(f"Final RSI: {metrics['rsi_value']}")
    print(f"Final BB Lower Distance: {metrics['bb_lower_dist']}")
    print(f"Final Reversion Reward: {metrics['reversion_reward']}")
    print(f"Price bounce: {(next_state[-1, 3] / state[-1, 3] - 1) * 100:.2f}%")
    
    # Verify the results
    assert metrics["reversion_reward"] > 0, f"Should get positive reversion reward, got {metrics['reversion_reward']}"
    assert metrics["rsi_value"] < 30, f"RSI should be oversold (<30), got {metrics['rsi_value']}"
    assert metrics["bb_lower_dist"] < 0.02, f"Price should be near BB lower band, got {metrics['bb_lower_dist']}"
    assert "bb_upper_dist" in metrics
```

```
// ... existing code ...
    def _calculate_rsi(self, prices: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        Calculate RSI using traditional approach.
        
        Args:
            prices: Array of price values
            
        Returns:
            RSI value between 0 and 100
        """
        if len(prices) < self.rsi_window + 1:
            return 50.0  # Return neutral RSI for insufficient data
        
        # Calculate price changes
        deltas = np.diff(prices[-self.rsi_window-1:])  # Use only the required window
        
        # Separate gains and losses
        gains = np.zeros_like(deltas)
        losses = np.zeros_like(deltas)
        
        # Calculate gains and losses
        gains[deltas > 0] = deltas[deltas > 0]
        losses[deltas < 0] = -deltas[deltas < 0]
        
        # Calculate average gains and losses
        avg_gain = np.mean(gains)
        avg_loss = np.mean(losses)
        
        # Handle edge cases
        if avg_loss == 0:
            if avg_gain == 0:
                return 50.0  # No movement
            return 100.0  # Only gains
        
        # Calculate RSI
        rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
        rsi = 100.0 - (100.0 / (1.0 + rs))
        
        # Ensure RSI stays within bounds
        rsi = max(0.0, min(100.0, rsi))
        
        return float(rsi)
// ... existing code ...
```

```
// ... existing code ...
    def train_step(self, state: np.ndarray, action: np.ndarray, 
                  reward: float, next_state: np.ndarray, 
                  done: bool) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Train the agent on a single state transition with mean reversion considerations.
        
        Args:
            state: Current state
            action: Action taken
            reward: Reward received
            next_state: Next state
            done: Whether episode is done
            
        Returns:
            Dictionary of training metrics
        """
        state_reversion = self._calculate_reversion_features(state)
        next_state_reversion = self._calculate_reversion_features(next_state)
        
        if len(state.shape) == 3:  # (batch, window, features)
            batch_size = state.shape[0]
            flat_state = state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            flat_next_state = next_state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, state_reversion], axis=1)
            augmented_next_state = np.concatenate([flat_next_state, next_state_reversion], axis=1)
        else:  # (window, features)
            flat_state = state.reshape(1, -1)
            flat_next_state = next_state.reshape(1, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, state_reversion.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
            augmented_next_state = np.concatenate([flat_next_state, next_state_reversion.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
        
        # Add mean reversion based reward modification
        reversion_reward = 0.0
        if len(state_reversion.shape) > 1:
            rsi = state_reversion[:, 0]
            bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[:, 1]
            bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[:, 2]
            
            # Calculate price movement
            current_prices = state[..., 3]
            next_prices = next_state[..., 3]
            price_changes = (next_prices - current_prices) / current_prices
            
            # Calculate mean reversion signals
            oversold_signal = (rsi < self.oversold_threshold) & (bb_lower_dist < 0.02)
            overbought_signal = (rsi > self.overbought_threshold) & (bb_upper_dist < 0.02)
            
            # Calculate rewards for different scenarios
            buy_reward = np.where(
                oversold_signal & (action > 0.2) & (price_changes > 0),
                0.5 * np.abs(price_changes) + 0.2,  # Strong reward for profitable buy
                np.where(
                    oversold_signal & (action > 0),
                    0.1,  # Small reward for correct direction
                    0.0
                )
            )
            
            sell_reward = np.where(
                overbought_signal & (action < -0.2) & (price_changes < 0),
                0.5 * np.abs(price_changes) + 0.2,  # Strong reward for profitable sell
                np.where(
                    overbought_signal & (action < 0),
                    0.1,  # Small reward for correct direction
                    0.0
                )
            )
            
            # Combine rewards
            reversion_reward = buy_reward + sell_reward
        else:
            rsi = state_reversion[0]
            bb_upper_dist = state_reversion[1]
            bb_lower_dist = state_reversion[2]
            
            # Calculate price movement
            current_price = state[..., 3][-1]
            next_price = next_state[..., 3][-1]
            price_change = (next_price - current_price) / current_price
            
            # Calculate rewards for mean reversion trades
            if rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.02:
                if action > 0.2 and price_change > 0:
                    reversion_reward = 0.5 * abs(price_change) + 0.2  # Strong reward for profitable buy
                elif action > 0:
                    reversion_reward = 0.1  # Small reward for correct direction
            elif rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.02:
                if action < -0.2 and price_change < 0:
                    reversion_reward = 0.5 * abs(price_change) + 0.2  # Strong reward for profitable sell
                elif action < 0:
                    reversion_reward = 0.1  # Small reward for correct direction
            
        modified_reward = reward + reversion_reward
        
        metrics = super().train_step(
            augmented_state.reshape(-1), action, modified_reward, augmented_next_state.reshape(-1), done
        )
        
        if metrics is None:
            return {
                "reversion_reward": 0.0,
                "rsi_value": float(rsi if isinstance(rsi, (int, float)) else rsi[0]),
                "bb_upper_dist": float(bb_upper_dist if isinstance(bb_upper_dist, (int, float)) else bb_upper_dist[0]),
                "bb_lower_dist": float(bb_lower_dist if isinstance(bb_lower_dist, (int, float)) else bb_lower_dist[0])
            }
        
        if len(state_reversion.shape) > 1:
            metrics.update({
                "reversion_reward": float(np.mean(reversion_reward)),
                "rsi_value": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 0])),
                "bb_upper_dist": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 1])),
                "bb_lower_dist": float(np.mean(state_reversion[:, 2]))
            })
        else:
            metrics.update({
                "reversion_reward": float(reversion_reward),
                "rsi_value": float(state_reversion[0]),
                "bb_upper_dist": float(state_reversion[1]),
                "bb_lower_dist": float(state_reversion[2])
            })
        
        return metrics
// ... existing code ...
```

```
def test_train_step_reward_modification(sample_config):
    agent = MeanReversionPPOAgent(sample_config)
    
    # Create a price series with initial stability followed by sharp decline
    state = np.zeros((20, 5))
    
    # Set initial stable period
    base_price = 100.0
    for i in range(10):
        state[i, 3] = base_price  # Flat prices
    
    # Add a period of small gains to establish baseline
    for i in range(5):
        state[10+i, 3] = base_price * (1.01 ** (i+1))  # Small gains
    
    # Then create a very sharp decline (each step down 15%)
    peak_price = state[14, 3]
    for i in range(5):
        state[15+i, 3] = peak_price * (0.85 ** (i+1))  # Each step down 15%
    
    # Ensure OHLC values are consistent and add some volatility
    for i in range(20):
        daily_volatility = state[i, 3] * 0.02  # 2% daily volatility
        state[i, 0] = state[i, 3] + daily_volatility  # High
        state[i, 1] = state[i, 3] - daily_volatility  # Low
        state[i, 2] = state[i, 3]  # Open same as close for simplicity
        state[i, 4] = 1000000  # Constant volume
    
    next_state = state.copy()
    # Price bounces back significantly (mean reversion)
    bounce_pct = 0.15  # 15% bounce
    next_state[-1, 3] *= (1.0 + bounce_pct)
    next_state[-1, 0] = next_state[-1, 3] * 1.02  # High
    next_state[-1, 1] = next_state[-1, 3] * 0.98  # Low
    next_state[-1, 2] = next_state[-1, 3]  # Open
    
    # Print initial debug information
    features = agent._calculate_reversion_features(state)
    print(f"Initial RSI: {features[0]}")
    print(f"Initial BB Upper Distance: {features[1]}")
    print(f"Initial BB Lower Distance: {features[2]}")
    print(f"Price series: {state[:, 3]}")
    print(f"Last 5 price changes (%): {[(state[i+1, 3] / state[i, 3] - 1) * 100 for i in range(-6, -1)]}")
    
    # Test reward modification for strong mean reversion trade
    metrics = agent.train_step(
        state=state,
        action=np.array([0.8]),  # Very strong buy in oversold (> 0.2 threshold)
        reward=0.1,
        next_state=next_state,
        done=False
    )

    print(f"Final BB Lower Distance: {metrics['bb_lower_dist']}")

    assert metrics["bb_lower_dist"] < 0.02, f"Price should be near BB lower band, got {metrics['bb_lower_dist']}"
    assert "bb_upper_dist" in metrics
```

```
    def get_action(self, state: np.ndarray, deterministic: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
        reversion_features = self._calculate_reversion_features(state)
        
        if len(state.shape) == 3:  # (batch, window, features)
            batch_size = state.shape[0]
            flat_state = state.reshape(batch_size, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, reversion_features], axis=1)
        else:  # (window, features)
            flat_state = state.reshape(1, -1)
            augmented_state = np.concatenate([flat_state, reversion_features.reshape(1, -1)], axis=1)
        
        # Get base action from policy network
        base_action = super().get_action(augmented_state.reshape(-1), deterministic)
        
        # Apply mean reversion based action modification
        if len(reversion_features.shape) > 1:
            rsi = reversion_features[:, 0]
            bb_upper_dist = reversion_features[:, 1]
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[:, 2]
            
            # Calculate mean reversion signals with stricter conditions
            oversold_signal = (rsi < self.oversold_threshold) & (bb_lower_dist < 0.01)
            overbought_signal = (rsi > self.overbought_threshold) & (bb_upper_dist < 0.01)
            
            # Calculate action bias based on signals
            action_bias = np.zeros_like(base_action)
            action_bias[oversold_signal] = 1.0  # Strong buy bias
            action_bias[overbought_signal] = -1.0  # Strong sell bias
            
            # Blend base action with bias (increased bias weight)
            action = 0.9 * action_bias + 0.1 * base_action
            
            # Ensure action stays within bounds
            action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0)
        else:
            rsi = reversion_features[0]
            bb_upper_dist = reversion_features[1]
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[2]
            
            # Calculate action bias based on signals with stricter conditions
            if rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.01:
                action_bias = 1.0  # Strong buy bias
            elif rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.01:
                action_bias = -1.0  # Strong sell bias
            else:
                action_bias = 0.0
            
            # Blend base action with bias (increased bias weight)
            action = 0.9 * action_bias + 0.1 * base_action[0]
            
            # Ensure action stays within bounds and wrap in array
            action = np.array([np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0)])
            
        return action
```

```
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[:, 2]
            
            # Calculate mean reversion signals
            oversold_signal = (rsi < self.oversold_threshold) & (bb_lower_dist < 0.02)
            overbought_signal = (rsi > self.overbought_threshold) & (bb_upper_dist < 0.02)
            
            # Calculate action bias based on signals
            action_bias = np.zeros_like(base_action)
            action_bias[oversold_signal] = 1.0  # Strong buy bias
            action_bias[overbought_signal] = -1.0  # Strong sell bias
            
            # Blend base action with bias (more weight on bias when signal is strong)
            signal_strength = np.where(oversold_signal | overbought_signal, 0.9, 0.1)
            action = signal_strength * action_bias + (1 - signal_strength) * base_action
            
            # Ensure action stays within bounds
            action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0)
        else:
            rsi = reversion_features[0]
            bb_upper_dist = reversion_features[1]
            bb_lower_dist = reversion_features[2]
            
            # Calculate action bias based on signals
            if rsi < self.oversold_threshold and bb_lower_dist < 0.02:
                action_bias = 1.0  # Strong buy bias
                signal_strength = 0.9
            elif rsi > self.overbought_threshold and bb_upper_dist < 0.02:
                action_bias = -1.0  # Strong sell bias
                signal_strength = 0.9
            else:
                action_bias = 0.0
                signal_strength = 0.1
            
            # Blend base action with bias (more weight on bias when signal is strong)
            action = signal_strength * action_bias + (1 - signal_strength) * base_action[0]
            
            # Ensure action stays within bounds and wrap in array
            action = np.array([np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0)])
            
        return action
// ... existing code ...
```

```
// ... existing code ...
    def _calculate_rsi(self, prices: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        Calculate RSI using traditional approach with Wilder's smoothing.
        
        Args:
            prices: Array of price values
            
        Returns:
            RSI value between 0 and 100
        """
        if len(prices) < self.rsi_window + 1:
            return 50.0  # Return neutral RSI for insufficient data
        
        # Calculate price changes
        deltas = np.diff(prices)
        
        # Get the window we'll use for calculation
        window_deltas = deltas[-self.rsi_window:]
        
        # Separate gains and losses
        gains = np.zeros_like(window_deltas)
        losses = np.zeros_like(window_deltas)
        
        gains[window_deltas > 0] = window_deltas[window_deltas > 0]
        losses[window_deltas < 0] = -window_deltas[window_deltas < 0]
        
        # Calculate initial averages
        avg_gain = gains[0]  # First value
        avg_loss = losses[0]  # First value
        
        # Update averages using Wilder's smoothing
        for i in range(1, len(window_deltas)):
            avg_gain = (avg_gain * (self.rsi_window - 1) + gains[i]) / self.rsi_window
            avg_loss = (avg_loss * (self.rsi_window - 1) + losses[i]) / self.rsi_window
        
        # Calculate final RSI
        if avg_loss == 0:
            return 100.0 if avg_gain > 0 else 50.0
        
        rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
        rsi = 100.0 - (100.0 / (1.0 + rs))
        
        return float(np.clip(rsi, 0.0, 100.0))
// ... existing code ...
```

```
    def _calculate_rsi(self, prices: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """
        Calculate RSI using traditional approach with Wilder's smoothing.
        
        Args:
            prices: Array of price values
            
        Returns:
            RSI value between 0 and 100
        """
        if len(prices) < self.rsi_window + 1:
            return 50.0  # Return neutral RSI for insufficient data
        
        # Calculate price changes
        deltas = np.diff(prices)
        
        # Get the window we'll use for calculation
        window_deltas = deltas[-self.rsi_window:]  # Use last rsi_window changes
        
        # Calculate gains and losses
        gains = np.maximum(window_deltas, 0)
        losses = -np.minimum(window_deltas, 0)
        
        # Calculate smoothed averages
        avg_gain = np.sum(gains) / self.rsi_window
        avg_loss = np.sum(losses) / self.rsi_window
        
        # Calculate final RSI
        if avg_loss == 0:
            return 100.0 if avg_gain > 0 else 50.0
        
        rs = avg_gain / avg_loss
        rsi = 100.0 - (100.0 / (1.0 + rs))
        
        return float(np.clip(rsi, 0.0, 100.0))
```

```
// ... existing code ...
    def _calculate_reversion_features(self, state: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Calculate mean reversion specific features from the state.
        
        Args:
            state: Raw state observation
            
        Returns:
            Mean reversion features as numpy array
        """
        if len(state.shape) == 1:
            state = state.reshape(1, -1)
        
        if state.shape[-1] >= 4:  # Ensure we have enough features
            if len(state.shape) == 3:  # (batch, window, features)
                close_prices = state[..., 3]  # Get close prices for all batches
            else:  # (window, features)
                close_prices = state[:, 3]  # Get close prices for single sample
            
            if len(close_prices.shape) == 2:  # Batch processing
                rsi_values = []
                bb_upper_values = []
                bb_lower_values = []
                
                for prices in close_prices:
                    rsi = self._calculate_rsi(prices)
                    bb_upper, bb_lower = self._calculate_bollinger_bands(prices)
                    current_price = prices[-1]
                    
                    rsi_values.append(rsi)
                    bb_upper_values.append((bb_upper - current_price) / current_price)
                    bb_lower_values.append((current_price - bb_lower) / current_price)
                
                rsi = np.array(rsi_values)
                bb_upper_dist = np.array(bb_upper_values)
                bb_lower_dist = np.array(bb_lower_values)
            else:  # Single sample processing
                rsi = self._calculate_rsi(close_prices)
                bb_upper, bb_lower = self._calculate_bollinger_bands(close_prices)
                current_price = close_prices[-1]
                bb_upper_dist = (bb_upper - current_price) / current_price
                bb_lower_dist = (current_price - bb_lower) / current_price
            
            return np.column_stack([rsi, bb_upper_dist, bb_lower_dist]) if len(state.shape) > 2 else np.array([rsi, bb_upper_dist, bb_lower_dist])
        else:
            shape = (state.shape[0], 3) if len(state.shape) > 2 else (3,)
            return np.zeros(shape, dtype=np.float32)
// ... existing code ...
```
